SLSQP on a for LSE beta:
```
def lse_opt(a, f_min, iters=200, beta_list=(20,80,250)):
  for beta in beta_list:
     def fun_g(a):
        a=np.asarray(a)
        c=np.convolve(a,a)
        m=c.max()
        z=np.exp(beta*(c-m))
        l=m + np.log(z.sum())/beta
        z/=z.sum()
        g=2*corr(z,a)
        return l, g
     res=minimize(fun_g, a, method='SLSQP', bounds=[(0,1)]*n, constraints=[{'type':'eq','fun':lambda x:x.sum()-1,'jac':lambda x:np.ones(n)}], options={'maxiter': iters, 'ftol':1e-9})
     a=res.x
```
Potential issue: fun_g called with a not simplex? constraint enforces but during iterations maybe slight. If sum not 1, scale? Since objective scale depends on sum; constraint should hold. For safety, in fun normalize? But then gradient mismatch. We can add if s<1e-8 return inf. We can also project a to simplex before computations and gradient adjust? SLSQP expects exact constraint. Better not normalize; just let SLSQP satisfy. At initial a sum1.
But bounds (0,1) and sum1 fine. The LSE value is homogeneous degree2 (since c degree2), objective not fixed scale; SLSQP equality fixes scale. Good.
Beta values: If c max ~1/n=0.01 for n=100, beta=100 means z over c differences; good. For small differences, use beta = max(20, 1/M*? maybe 10/M?). If M=0.005, 1/M=200. Implement central generator:
```
def central_seq(n):
  a=np.empty(n); a[0]=1.0
  for k in range(1,n): a[k]=a[k-1]*(2*k-1)/(2*k)
  return a / a.sum()
```
For n large, values no underflow (a_n ~1/√n ~0.011? unnormalized; normalized small). Good.

Projection simplex:
```
def project_simplex(v):
    n=v.size
    if n==1: return np.array([1.0]) if v[0]>=0 else? ensure sum1.
    w=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cum=np.cumsum(w)
    wc=1/np.arange(1,n+1)
    v=v.cumsum()+1? Standard:
    t = np.arange(1,n+1)
    ind = (w - (cum - 1)*wc?) Let's use known:
    def proj(v):
        u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
        cssv = np.cumsum(u)
        cond = u * np.arange(1,n+1) > (cssv - 1)
        k = cond.nonzero()[0][-1]
        theta = cssv[k]/(k+1)
        return np.maximum(v - theta, 0)
    a=proj(v); a/=a.sum()
```
Check `cond = u * np.arange(1,n+1) > (cssv - 1)`. If v=[1,1,0], u[1,1,0], cssv[1,2,2], t[1,2,3]; cond:1>0 true,2>1 true,0>1 false ->k=1, theta=2/2=1, a=[0,0,0]? Wait v-theta=[0,0,-1], max [0,0,0] bad. Standard formula uses `cssv - 1` and `u * t > cssv - 1`, k index count. For v=[1,1,0], projection should [0.5,0.5,0]. u* t: [1,2,0], cssv-1: [0,1,1]; cond [True, True, False] -> k=1? nonzeros [0,1], last 1, theta=cssv[1]/(1+1)=2/2=1 -> wrong. Maybe theta = (cssv[k] - 1)/(k+1) = (2-1)/2=0.5. Yes formula: theta=(cum[k]-1)/(k+1). Use that. Good.

def pgrad_search(a, n, deadline, step=0.02, q0=8, q_end=64, max_no_improve=200):
   current=score
   best=current
   no=0
   it=0
   while time<deadline:
      it+=1
      q = q0 + (q_end-q0)*min(1,it/200)
      c=conv; M=c.max(); score=...
      if score<best: best=score; no=0 else no+=1
      if M==0 or no>500: break
      w = np.exp(q*(c-M)/M)  # M>0
      w /= w.sum()
      g=2*np.convolve(a, w[::-1])[::-1][:n]
      h=g-g.mean(); maxh=max(abs(h)); if maxh<1e-15 break
      base=step/maxh
      best_score_candidate=score; best_a_candidate=a
      for factor in [1.0,0.5,0.25,2.0,0.1,4.0]:
          cand=project_simplex(a - base*factor*h)
          sc=score_fast(cand,n)
          if sc<best_score_candidate: ...
      if best_score_candidate < current - 1e-12:
          a=best_candidate; current=best_score_candidate
          if current<global_best...
          if random? step*=1.05? maybe keep
      else:
          step *= 0.5; no+=5
          if step < 1e-6: break
          # occasionally random nudge
```

```
import numpy as np, time, math
from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize

def _normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    a = a/s
    return a
```

`lp_direction(a, M)`:
```
n=a.size
A = np.zeros((2*n-1, n+1), dtype=np.float64)
for k in range(2*n-1):
    j0=max(0,k-n+1); j1=min(n-1,k)
    if j1>=j0:
       idx=np.arange(j0,j1+1)
       A[k, idx]=a[k-idx]
A[:,n]=-0.5
c=np.zeros(n+1); c[n]=1.0
Aeq=np.zeros((1,n+1)); Aeq[0,:n]=1.0
bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
bounds.append((-2.0*M,0.0))
try:
  res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2*n-1,M), A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=[1.0], bounds=bounds, method='highs')
except: return None
if not res.success: return None
tau=float(res.x[n]); b=res.x[:n]
if tau > -1e-7: return None
b=np.clip(b,0,1); s=b.sum(); if s<1e-12 return None
return b/s, tau
```
The lower bound for tau -2M? Since τ derivative bound; with b=0? But sum b=1. More negative than -2M impossible. Bounds okay. If M small, -2M ok. But due to feasible at τ=0, ub=0. If linprog returns infeasible due to numerical, handle.

`lse_polish(a, n, deadline, max_iters=?)`: Use SLSQP for few beta. But SLSQP may take time. Implement with short maxiter and time check inside? Objective doesn't have time. We'll call with maxiter small (e.g., 100) and hope.
```
def lse_polish(a,n,deadline):
  a=normalize(a).copy(); current=score_fast(a)
  for beta in [10,40,150,500]:
    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2: break
    def fun_g(a):
       a=np.asarray(a)
       if a.size!=n: return inf, None
       a=np.clip(a,0,1)
       c=np.convolve(a,a)
       m=float(c.max())
       if m<=0: return 0, np.zeros(n)
       u=np.clip(c-m, None, 0.0) * beta
       # if beta too large, exp underflow ok
       z=np.exp(u)
       s=z.sum()
       val=m + np.log(s)/beta if s>0 else m
       if s==0: return val, np.zeros(n)
       w=z/s
       g=2*np.convolve(a, w[::-1])[::-1][:n]
       return val,g
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':lambda x: x.sum()-1.0, 'jac':lambda x: np.ones(x.size)}]
    try:
       res = minimize(fun_g, a, jac=True, method='SLSQP', bounds=[(0,1)]*n, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':80, 'ftol':1e-10})
       if res.success and res.x is not None:
          cand=normalize(res.x)
          if cand is not None:
             sc=score_fast(cand)
             if sc<current: a=cand; current=sc
    except: pass
  return a,current
```
Beta should scale with M? If M~1e-4, beta=10 gives weights not peaked; beta=500 gives e^{500*diff}. Differences maybe 1e-5, exponent -5, okay. For small M maybe use `beta = max(10, min(2000, 20/(M*a.size?)))`? Actually c-m relative; use beta = 20*(n)/(?) No, (c-m)/M in [-1,0], so beta controls number of effective lags: e^{-beta}. beta=20 means lags with deficit >0.05M downweighted. Good independent of M. Use list [5,20,100,400].
Potential overflow if c-m positive tiny from round: clip to 0.
SLSQP with bounds (0,1) and sum1; if res.x slightly violates, normalize.

`pgrad_search(a,n,deadline)`:
Used for large n and final.
```
def pgrad_search(a,n,deadline,step=0.02,q0=4,qmax=32):
  a=normalize(a).copy()
  current=score_fast(a); best_score=current; best_a=a.copy(); no=0; it=0
  base_step=step
  while time < deadline:
    it+=1
    if it % 50 ==0 and time>deadline-10: break
    c=np.convolve(a,a); M=float(c.max()); score=float(2*n*M/(a.sum()**2)) # should 1
    if score<best_score: best_score=score; best_a=a.copy(); no=0
    else: no+=1
    if no>500 or M<=0: break
    q = q0 + (qmax-q0)*min(1.0, it/200)
    w = np.exp(q*(c-M)/M)
    wsum=w.sum(); if wsum==0 or not finite: break
    w/=wsum
    g=2*np.convolve(a,w[::-1])[::-1][:n]
    h=g-g.mean(); maxh=float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
    if maxh<1e-14: break
    base = base_step / maxh
    cand_best_score=score; cand_best_a=a
    factors=[1.0,0.5,0.25,2.0,0.1,4.0]
    for f in factors:
       if base*f > 0.5? projection handles but maybe too large.
       cand=project_simplex(a - base*f*h)
       sc=score_fast(cand)
       if sc<cand_best_score:
          cand_best_score=sc; cand_best_a=cand
    if cand_best_score < current - 1e-12:
       a=cand_best_a; current=cand_best_score; no=0
       if current<best_score: best_score=current; best_a=a.copy()
       # increase step if factor 4 was best? skip
    else:
       base_step *= 0.6
       if base_step < 1e-5: break
       # occasionally random nudge
       if no % 50 ==0:
          rng=np.random.default_rng(12345+it)
          noise=np.exp(rng.normal(size=n)*0.01)
          cand=normalize(a*noise)
          sc=score_fast(cand)
          if sc<current:
             a=cand; current=sc; no=0
  return best_a,best_score
```
But `current` should be score of a; after best update, if candidate not better than current? We choose candidate best among trials and current, so cand_best_score ≤ current. Good.
The step scale base = step/maxh. If maxh very small, base huge; cap? We can if base>1e3 maybe set base=1e3? But projection handles, too big may zeros. Maybe set `base = min(1000.0, base)`.
Factors include 4 => change up to 4*step. Step initial0.02, max change0.08; okay. For n large, h max maybe? central h max ~? g max ~2/n =0.00032, step/maxh=0.02/0.00032=62, change~0.02. Good.
If step decreases to1e-5, no movement.

```
import numpy as np, time, math
from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize

def _norm(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
    if a.ndim!=1 or a.size==0: return None
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return None
    a=a/s
    return a
```
Could clipping to1000 after normalize not needed. If a has large values, normalize first? Evaluator max0 then min1000. Our sequences small.

`_score(a)`:
```
s=a.sum()
if s<1e-12: return inf
m=np.convolve(a,a).max()
return float(2*a.size*m/(s*s))
```

`_project_simplex(v)`:
```
v=np.asarray(v,float)
n=v.size
if n==1: return np.ones(1)
u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
css=np.cumsum(u)
t=np.arange(1,n+1)
cond = u*t > css-1
if not cond.any(): k=0
else: k=np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1]
theta=(css[k]-1)/(k+1)
out=np.maximum(v-theta,0)
out_sum=out.sum()
if out_sum<1e-12:
   # fallback uniform where v largest? 
   out=np.zeros(n); out[np.argmax(v)]=1.0
else: out/=out_sum
return out
```
For cond formula with u*t > css-1. For v=[1,1,0], t [1,2,3], u*t [1,2,0], css-1 [0,1,1], cond [T,T,F], k=1 theta=(2-1)/2=.5 good.

Central seq:
```
def _central(n):
  a=np.ones(n)
  val=1.0
  a[0]=1
  for k in range(1,n):
     val *= (2*k-1)/(2*k)
     a[k]=val
  s=a.sum(); return a/s
```

Other init:
```
def _power(n,p):
  x=np.arange(n)
  # a = 1/sqrt(k+1) maybe
  a = (x+1.0)**(-p)
  return _norm(a)
```
`_geometric(n, lam)`: a=np.exp(-lam*x/(n-1)) normalized.
`_linear(n)`: a=1 - x/(n-1) positive.
But maybe not useful. We can generate a few if time.

`optimize_lp(a,n,deadline,max_iters=200)`: We'll use `M = np.convolve(a,a).max()` from current. But `_score` recomputes. For efficiency, compute c and M once. Line search uses `_score` for candidates. Since n small, fine.

```
def _lp_improve(a,n,deadline):
  a=_norm(a).copy()
  maxiter = 300 if n<=128 else 150
  stale=0
  for it in range(maxiter):
    if time.monotonic()>deadline-5: break
    c=np.convolve(a,a); M=float(c.max())
    if M<=1e-14: break
    b,tau=_lp_dir(a,M)
    if b is None: break
    cur=2*n*M  # sum1
    best_sc=cur; best_a=a
    # include some t; if tau very negative maybe large t? use sorted by likely
    for t in (0.25,0.5,0.1,0.75,1.0,0.05,0.2,0.8,0.9):
       cand=(1-t)*a + t*b
       cand=_norm(cand)
       if cand is None: continue
       sc=_score(cand)
       if sc<best_sc:
          best_sc=sc; best_a=cand
    if best_a is not a and best_sc < cur - 1e-12:
       a=best_a; stale=0
    else:
       stale+=1
       if stale>15: break

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import linprog

def _norm(a): ...
def _score(a): ...
def _project_simplex(v): ...
def _central(n): ...
def _lp_dir(a,M): ...
def _lp_improve(a,n,limit): ...
def _pgrad(a,n,limit,step=0.02,q0=4,qmax=32): ...

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    deadline=t0+max(0.0,float(budget_s)-5.0)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_a=None; best_sc=float('inf')
    def consider(a): ...
    h=globals().get('height_sequence_1')
    n_parent=0
    if h is not None:
       try:
          hh=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
          if hh.ndim==1 and hh.size>0:
             n_parent=hh.size; consider(hh)
       except: pass
    if n_parent==0: n_parent=6121
    central_n = n_parent if n_parent>=1024 else 2048
    a=_central(central_n); consider(a)
    a=_central(256); consider(a)
    # candidate n list scaled by available time
    cand=[(24,8),(32,12),(48,18),(64,25),(80,25),(96,35),(128,45),(160,55),(200,70),(256,80)]
    # If budget_s small, scale
    total_c = 50 or min.
    for n,bud in cand:
       if time>deadline-120: break
       alloc = min(bud, max(5, float(budget_s)*0.04)) # maybe
       if time+alloc>deadline-120: alloc=max(5,deadline-120-time)
       if alloc<=2: continue
       a0=_central(n)
       # local optimize:
       lim = time.monotonic()+alloc
       if n<=300:
           a0,_=_lp_improve(a0,n,min(lim, time.monotonic()+alloc*0.6))
       a0,_=_pgrad(a0,n,lim)
       consider(a0)
       # random init for some n
       if n in (32,64,96) and time<deadline-60:
          a0=rng.dirichlet(np.ones(n))
          lim=time.monotonic()+min(15, deadline-60-time.monotonic())
          a0,_=_pgrad(a0,n,lim)
          consider(a0)
    # final pgrad on best
    if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline-10:
       n=best_a.size
       a,sc=_pgrad(best_a,n,deadline-5)
       consider(a)
    # if best still high and time remains, try parent central pgrad
    if best_sc > 1.52 and time.monotonic() < deadline-120:
       n = max(1024, min(n_parent, 8192)) if n_parent>=1024 else 2048
       a=_central(n)
       a,sc=_pgrad(a,n,deadline-5)
       consider(a)
    # validate
    if best_a is None:
       best_a=_central(128)
    lst=[float(x) for x in best_a]
    try:
       sc=evaluate_sequence(lst)
       if not math.isfinite(sc): raise ValueError
    except:
       lst=[float(x) for x in _central(128)]
    return lst
```

Double-check `_project_simplex` cond formula. From Wiki:
```
v = np.sort(v)[::-1]
cv = np.cumsum(v)
i = np.arange(1,n+1)
condition = v * i > cv - 1
k = condition.nonzero()[0][-1]
theta = cv[k] / (k+1)
u = np.maximum(v - theta, 0)
```

We want minimize ∑(u - (v_i-θ))^2 with u_i≥0 and sum=1. For support S size k+1, optimal θ = (∑_{i∈S} v_i -1)/(k+1). Condition for support: v_i > θ. cv[k] is sorted sum. So θ=(cv[k]-1)/(k+1). For [1,1,0], cv[1]=2 -> θ=0.5, output .5,.5. Thus use (cv-1). Condition: v_i > θ? The precomputed condition `v * i > cv - 1` equivalent v_i > (cv_i -1)/i. For i=2:2 > (2-1)/2=0.5 true. But if M is max over c and some c slightly >M due float? M=max so no.

In `_lp_dir`, A coefficients a, but constraints should be (a*b)_k -0.5τ ≤ M. We use b_ub=M. Good. If tau negative, RHS effectively M-|τ|/2 (since -0.5 tau moves). A row with -0.5τ: A_row b -0.5τ ≤M -> conv ≤ M +0.5τ? Wait A includes -0.5 in tau column. Row: ∑A_kj b_j + (-0.5)τ ≤ M. If τ negative, left = conv -0.5|τ| ≤M -> conv ≤ M +0.5|τ|? Actually -0.5τ = +0.5|τ|, so conv +0.5|τ| ≤ M => conv ≤ M -0.5|τ|. Good, matches. If we wanted conv ≤ M + τ/2, moving τ negative lowers. Good.

Directional derivative: τ bound = 2 max(conv -M). If conv ≤ M -0.5|τ|, derivative ≤ -|τ|. Good.

Now output.
</think>

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import linprog


def _norm(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return None
    return a / s


def _score(a):
    a = _norm(a)
    if a is None:
        return float("inf")
    c = np.convolve(a, a)
    m = float(c.max())
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12 or not math.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m / (s * s))


def _project_simplex(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = v.size
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css = np.cumsum(u)
    t = np.arange(1, n + 1)
    cond = u * t > css - 1.0
    if cond.any():
        k = int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1])
    else:
        k = 0
    theta = (css[k] - 1.0) / (k + 1)
    out = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        out = np.zeros(n)
        out[int(np.argmax(v))] = 1.0
        s = 1.0
    return out / s


def _central(n):
    a = np.empty(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    v = 1.0
    a[0] = 1.0
    for k in range(1, int(n)):
        v *= (2 * k - 1) / (2 * k)
        a[k] = v
    s = float(a.sum())
    return a / s


def _lp_dir(a, M):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n < 2 or M <= 0.0:
        return None, 0.0
    A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(2 * n - 1):
        s = max(0, k - n + 1)
        e = min(n - 1, k)
        if e >= s:
            A[k, s:e + 1] = a[k - e:k - s + 1][::-1]
    A[:, n] = -0.5
    c = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    c[n] = 1.0
    Aeq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    Aeq[0, :n] = 1.0
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n + [(-2.0 * M, 0.0)]
    try:
        res = linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=np.full(2 * n - 1, M, dtype=np.float64),
            A_eq=Aeq,
            b_eq=np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64),
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
        )
    except Exception:
        return None, 0.0
    if getattr(res, "success", False):
        b = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
        tau = float(res.x[n])
        if tau < -1e-9:
            b = np.clip(b, 0.0, 1.0)
            s = float(b.sum())
            if s > 1e-12:
                return b / s, tau
    return None, 0.0


def _lp_improve(a, n, limit):
    a = _norm(a).copy()
    n = int(n)
    stale = 0
    max_iter = 4000 if n <= 64 else (1500 if n <= 192 else 600)
    for _ in range(max_iter):
        if time.monotonic() > limit:
            break
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
        M = float(c.max())
        if M <= 1e-14:
            break
        b, tau = _lp_dir(a, M)
        if b is None:
            break
        cur = float(2.0 * n * M)
        best_a = a
        best_sc = cur
        for t in (0.25, 0.5, 0.1, 0.75, 1.0, 0.05, 0.2, 0.8):
            cand = (1.0 - t) * a + t * b
            cand = _norm(cand)
            if cand is None:
                continue
            sc = _score(cand)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = cand
        if best_sc < cur - 1e-12:
            a = best_a
            stale = 0
        else:
            stale += 1
            if stale > 15:
                break
    return a


def _pgrad(a, n, limit, step0=0.02, q0=4, qmax=32):
    a = _norm(a).copy()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return a
    cur = _score(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = cur
    no_imp = 0
    step = float(step0)
    it = 0
    qmax = max(32, int(qmax))
    while time.monotonic() < limit:
        it += 1
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
        M = float(c.max())
        s = float(a.sum())
        sc = float(2.0 * n * M / (s * s)) if s > 0 else float("inf")
        if not math.isfinite(sc):
            break
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            no_imp = 0
        else:
            no_imp += 1
        if M <= 1e-15:
            break
        if no_imp > 120:
            a = best_a.copy()
            sc = best_sc
            no_imp = 0
            step *= 0.7
        q = float(q0) + (float(qmax) - float(q0)) * min(1.0, it / 250.0)
        alpha = q / max(M, 1e-12)
        w = np.exp(alpha * np.clip(c - M, None, 0.0))
        ws = float(w.sum())
        if ws <= 1e-15 or not math.isfinite(ws):
            break
        w /= ws
        g = 2.0 * np.convolve(a, w[::-1])[::-1][:n]
        h = g - float(g.mean())
        mh = float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
        if mh <= 1e-15:
            break
        base = min(1000.0, step / mh)
        if n > 1000:
            factors = (1.0,)
        elif n > 300:
            factors = (1.0, 0.5)
        else:
            factors = (1.0, 0.5, 2.0, 0.25, 0.1)
        cand_a = a
        cand_sc = sc
        for f in factors:
            cand = _project_simplex(a - base * f * h)
            cc = _score(cand)
            if cc < cand_sc:
                cand_sc = cc
                cand_a = cand
        if cand_sc < sc - 1e-12:
            a = cand_a
            sc = cand_sc
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = a.copy()
            step = min(0.05, step * 1.03)
            no_imp = 0
        else:
            step *= 0.65
            if step < 1e-5:
                break
    return best_a


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best_a = None
    best_sc = float("inf")

    def consider(a):
        nonlocal best_a, best_sc
        a = _norm(a)
        if a is None:
            return
        sc = _score(a)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

    parent = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
    n_parent = 0
    if parent is not None:
        try:
            p = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64)
            if p.ndim == 1 and p.size > 0:
                n_parent = int(p.size)
                consider(p)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if n_parent == 0:
        n_parent = 6121

    n_large = n_parent if n_parent >= 1024 else 2048
    n_large = int(min(max(n_large, 1024), 12288))
    consider(_central(n_large))
    consider(_central(256))

    plan = [
        (24, 8), (32, 12), (48, 18), (64, 25), (80, 25), (96, 35),
        (128, 45), (160, 55), (200, 70), (256, 80), (384, 100), (512, 120),
    ]
    scale = max(0.2, min(1.0, float(budget_s) / 900.0))

    for n, alloc in plan:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120:
            break
        alloc = float(alloc) * scale
        if alloc < 3.0:
            alloc = 3.0
        rem = deadline - 120.0 - time.monotonic()
        if rem <= 2.0:
            break
        alloc = min(alloc, rem)
        now = time.monotonic()

        a = _central(n)
        lp_limit = now + min(alloc * 0.6, 60.0 if n <= 128 else 90.0)
        if n <= 300:
            a = _lp_improve(a, n, lp_limit)
        a = _pgrad(a, n, now + alloc)
        consider(a)

        if n in (32, 64, 96) and time.monotonic() < deadline - 60:
            ar = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(n))
            ar = _pgrad(ar, n, min(deadline - 30.0, time.monotonic() + 15.0 * scale))
            consider(ar)

    if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 10:
        a = _pgrad(best_a, best_a.size, deadline - 5.0)
        consider(a)

    if best_sc > 1.52 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 120:
        n_try = max(1024, min(n_parent, 8192)) if n_parent >= 1024 else 2048
        a = _central(int(n_try))
        a = _pgrad(a, a.size, deadline - 5.0)
        consider(a)

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = _central(128)

    out = [float(x) for x in best_a]
    try:
        sc = evaluate_sequence(out)
        if not math.isfinite(sc):
            out = [float(x) for x in _central(128)]
    except Exception:
        out = [float(x) for x in _central(128)]

    return out
```
